# P7 milestone01 evidence archive

The manifest records every preserved file SHA-256 and original path. Large synthetic Markdown fixtures and native run samples.json use deterministic lossless gzip; original byte counts and hashes remain verifiable. Raw failures are retained. This index records provenance, not a declaration that every acceptance target passed.

Excluded categories: application ELF/build artifacts, dependencies, unreviewed runtime members/profile trees, hidden recovery work files, locks, unpacked bundle duplicates and sensitive filenames. The source evidence is not removed or modified.
